From handbook line 214\nPREHEAT_START_TEMP_C = 100  # From handbook line 212\nPREHEAT_END_TEMP_C = 150    # From handbook line 212\nMIN_TAL_S = 30  # From handbook line 231\nMAX_TAL_S = 60  # From handbook line 231\nMIN_PEAK_OFFSET_C = 20  # From handbook line 228\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load all data files.\"\"\"\n    mes_log = pd.read_csv('/app/data/mes_log.csv')\n    thermocouples = pd.read_csv('/app/data/thermocouples.csv')\n    test_defects = pd.read_csv('/app/data/test_defects.csv')\n    return mes_log, thermocouples, test_defects\n\ndef calculate_preheat_ramp_rates(thermocouples_df: pd.DataFrame, mes_log_df: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 1: Calculate maximum preheat ramp rate for each run.\n\n    Preheat zone is defined as 100°C to 150°C (from handbook).\n    Ramp rate = ΔT / Δt in °C/second\n    Select thermocouple at \"largest_mass\" location (most representative per handbook).\n    \"\"\"\n    results = {\n        \"ramp_rate_limit_c_per_s\": PREHEAT_RAMP_LIMIT_C_PER_S,\n        \"violating_runs\": [],\n        \"max_ramp_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    # Get unique run IDs\n    run_ids = sorted(thermocouples_df['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in run_ids:\n        run_data = thermocouples_df[thermocouples_df['run_id'] == run_id].copy()\n\n        # Select thermocouple at largest_mass location (most representative)\n        largest_mass_data = run_data[run_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n        if largest_mass_data.empty:\n            # If no largest_mass TC, skip this run\n            continue\n\n        # Get the first TC ID (should be consistent for a location)\n        tc_id = largest_mass_data['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n\n        # Filter data in preheat range (100°C to 150°C)\n        preheat_data = largest_mass_data[\n            (largest_mass_data['temp_c'] >= PREHEAT_START_TEMP_C) &\n            (largest_mass_data['temp_c'] <= PREHEAT_END_TEMP_C)\n        ].sort_values('time_s')\n\n        if len(preheat_data) < 2:\n            # Not enough data in preheat range\n            continue\n\n        # Calculate ramp rates between consecutive points\n        max_ramp = 0.0\n        for i in range(1, len(preheat_data)):\n            delta_t_s = preheat_data.iloc[i]['time_s'] - preheat_data.iloc[i-1]['time_s']\n            if delta_t_s > 0:\n                delta_temp_c = preheat_data.iloc[i]['temp_c'] - preheat_data.iloc[i-1]['temp_c']\n                ramp_rate = delta_temp_c / delta_t_s\n                max_ramp = max(max_ramp, ramp_rate)\n\n        results[\"max_ramp_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n            \"max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s\": round(max_ramp, 2)\n        }\n\n        # Check if violating\n        if max_ramp > PREHEAT_RAMP_LIMIT_C_PER_S:\n            results[\"violating_runs\"].append(run_id)\n\n    # Sort violating runs\n    results[\"violating_runs\"].sort()\n\n    return results\n\ndef calculate_time_above_liquidus(thermocouples_df: pd.DataFrame, mes_log_df: pd.DataFrame) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 2: Calculate time above liquidus (TAL) for each run.\n\n    TAL is the time spent above the solder liquidus temperature.\n    From handbook: wetting time is normally 30-60 seconds (lines 230-231).\n    Liquidus temperature is obtained from mes_log (solder_liquidus_c).\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n\n    # Get unique run IDs\n    run_ids = sorted(thermocouples_df['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in run_ids:\n        # Get liquidus temperature for this run\n        run_mes = mes_log_df[mes_log_df['run_id'] == run_id]\n        if run_mes.empty:\n            continue\n\n        liquidus_c = run_mes['solder_liquidus_c'].iloc[0]\n\n        # Get thermocouple data\n        run_data = thermocouples_df[thermocouples_df['run_id'] == run_id].copy()\n\n        # Select thermocouple at largest_mass location (most representative)\n        largest_mass_data = run_data[run_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n        if largest_mass_data.empty:\n            continue\n\n        tc_id = largest_mass_data['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n        largest_mass_data = largest_mass_data.sort_values('time_s')\n\n        # Find time above liquidus\n        above_liquidus = largest_mass_data[largest_mass_data['temp_c'] > liquidus_c]\n\n        if len(above_liquidus) < 2:\n            tal_s = 0.0\n        else:\n            # TAL is the time from first crossing to last crossing\n            tal_s = above_liquidus['time_s'].max() - above_liquidus['time_s'].min()\n\n        # Determine compliance\n        status = \"compliant\" if (MIN_TAL_S <= tal_s <= MAX_TAL_S) else \"non-compliant\"\n\n        results.append({\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n            \"tal_s\": round(tal_s, 2),\n            \"required_min_tal_s\": MIN_TAL_S,\n            \"required_max_tal_s\": MAX_TAL_S,\n            \"status\": status\n        })\n\n    return results\n\ndef check_peak_temperature(thermocouples_df: pd.DataFrame, mes_log_df: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 3: Check if each run meets peak temperature requirement.\n\n    From handbook: temperature must exceed melting point by ~20°C (line 228).\n    Minimum peak = liquidus + 20°C\n    \"\"\"\n    results = {\n        \"failing_runs\": [],\n        \"min_peak_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    # Get all run IDs from mes_log\n    all_run_ids = sorted(mes_log_df['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_run_ids:\n        # Get liquidus temperature for this run\n        run_mes = mes_log_df[mes_log_df['run_id'] == run_id]\n        liquidus_c = run_mes['solder_liquidus_c'].iloc[0]\n        required_min_peak = liquidus_c + MIN_PEAK_OFFSET_C\n\n        # Get thermocouple data\n        run_data = thermocouples_df[thermocouples_df['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if run_data.empty:\n            # No thermocouple data = failing\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": None,\n                \"peak_temp_c\": None,\n                \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n            }\n            continue\n\n        # Select thermocouple at largest_mass location\n        largest_mass_data = run_data[run_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n        if largest_mass_data.empty:\n            # No largest_mass TC = failing\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": None,\n                \"peak_temp_c\": None,\n                \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n            }\n            continue\n\n        tc_id = largest_mass_data['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n        peak_temp = largest_mass_data['temp_c'].max()\n\n        results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n            \"peak_temp_c\": round(peak_temp, 2),\n            \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n        }\n\n        # Check if failing\n        if peak_temp < required_min_peak:\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n\n    return results\n\ndef check_conveyor_speed(mes_log_df: pd.DataFrame) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 4: Check if conveyor speed is feasible.\n\n    From handbook page 14-15 (lines 874-893):\n    LINE SPEED (MINIMUM) = BOARDS PER MINUTE × LENGTH PER BOARD / LOAD FACTOR\n    PROCESS SPEED = OVEN CHAMBER HEATED LENGTH / PROCESS DWELL TIME\n\n    The actual conveyor speed must be >= minimum line speed to avoid jams.\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n\n    # Need to determine required minimum speed based on thermal dwell requirements\n    # From handbook: typical process with 3 zones (preheat, dryout, reflow)\n    # Total dwell time can be estimated from the thermocouple data\n\n    for _, row in mes_log_df.iterrows():\n        run_id = row['run_id']\n\n        # Get board geometry\n        board_length_cm = row['board_length_cm']\n        boards_per_min = row['boards_per_min']\n        loading_factor = row['loading_factor']\n        actual_speed_cm_min = row['conveyor_speed_cm_min']\n\n        # Calculate minimum required conveyor speed\n        # This is based on preventing board jams at the entrance\n        required_min_speed = (boards_per_min * board_length_cm) / loading_factor\n\n        # Check if actual speed meets requirement\n        meets = actual_speed_cm_min >= required_min_speed\n\n        results.append({\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"required_min_speed_cm_min\": round(required_min_speed, 2),\n            \"actual_speed_cm_min\": round(actual_speed_cm_min, 2),\n            \"meets\": meets\n        })\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    return results\n\ndef select_best_runs(mes_log_df: pd.DataFrame, test_defects_df: pd.DataFrame,\n                     thermocouples_df: pd.DataFrame) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 5: Select best run per board_family based on quality and efficiency.\n\n    Criteria for \"best\":\n    1. Quality: highest yield (fp_yield_pct from test_defects SUMMARY rows)\n    2. Efficiency: fastest conveyor speed (secondary criterion)\n    3. Process compliance: meets temperature requirements\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n\n    # Get yield data from test defects\n    yield_data = test_defects_df[test_defects_df['inspection_stage'] == 'SUMMARY'][\n        ['run_id', 'fp_yield_pct']\n    ].copy()\n\n    # Merge with mes_log to get board_family and conveyor_speed\n    merged = mes_log_df.merge(yield_data, on='run_id', how='left')\n\n    # Group by board_family\n    board_families = sorted(merged['board_family'].unique())\n\n    for board_family in board_families:\n        family_runs = merged[merged['board_family'] == board_family].copy()\n\n        # Fill NaN yields with 0 (if no test data)\n        family_runs['fp_yield_pct'] = family_runs['fp_yield_pct'].fillna(0)\n\n        # Sort by:\n        # 1. Yield (descending)\n        # 2. Conveyor speed (descending - faster is better for efficiency)\n        family_runs = family_runs.sort_values(\n            by=['fp_yield_pct', 'conveyor_speed_cm_min'],\n            ascending=[False, False]\n        )\n\n        # Best run is the first one\n        best_run_id = family_runs.iloc[0]['run_id']\n\n        # All other runs are runner-ups\n        runner_up_run_ids = sorted(family_runs.iloc[1:]['run_id'].tolist())\n\n        results.append({\n            \"board_family\": board_family,\n            \"best_run_id\": best_run_id,\n            \"runner_up_run_ids\": runner_up_run_ids\n        })\n\n    # Sort by board_family\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['board_family'])\n\n    return results\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading data...\")\n    mes_log, thermocouples, test_defects = load_data()\n\n    print(\"Processing Question 1: Preheat ramp rates...\")\n    q1_results = calculate_preheat_ramp_rates(thermocouples, mes_log)\n    with open('/app/output/q01.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q1_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"  Found {len(q1_results['violating_runs'])} runs violating ramp rate limit\")\n\n    print(\"Processing Question 2: Time above liquidus...\")\n    q2_results = calculate_time_above_liquidus(thermocouples, Question 3: Peak temperature requirements...\")\n    q3_results = check_peak_temperature(thermocouples, mes_log)\n    with open('/app/output/q03.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q3_results, f, print(f\"  Found {failing_speed} runs with insufficient conveyor speed\")\n\n    print(\"Processing Question 5: Best run selection...\")\n    q5_results = select_best_runs(mes_log, test_defects, thermocouples)\n    with open('/app/output/q05.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q5_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"  Selected best runs for {len(q5_results)} board families\")\n\n    print(\"\\nAll questions processed successfully!\")\n    print(\"Output files written to /app/output/\")\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n From handbook line 214\nPREHEAT_START_TEMP_C = 100  # From handbook line 212\nPREHEAT_END_TEMP_C = 150    # From handbook line 212\nMIN_TAL_S = 30  # From handbook line 231\nMAX_TAL_S = 60  # From handbook line 231\nMIN_PEAK_OFFSET_C = 20  # From handbook line 228\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load all data files.\"\"\"\n    mes_log = pd.read_csv('/app/data/mes_log.csv')\n    thermocouples = pd.read_csv('/app/data/thermocouples.csv')\n    test_defects = pd.read_csv('/app/data/test_defects.csv')\n    return mes_log, thermocouples, test_defects\n\ndef calculate_preheat_ramp_rates(thermocouples_df: pd.DataFrame, mes_log_df: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 1: Calculate maximum preheat ramp rate for each run.\n\n    Preheat zone is defined as 100°C to 150°C (from handbook).\n    Ramp rate = ΔT / Δt in °C/second\n    Select thermocouple at \"largest_mass\" location (most representative per handbook).\n    \"\"\"\n    results = {\n        \"ramp_rate_limit_c_per_s\": PREHEAT_RAMP_LIMIT_C_PER_S,\n        \"violating_runs\": [],\n        \"max_ramp_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    # Get unique run IDs\n    run_ids = sorted(thermocouples_df['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in run_ids:\n        run_data = thermocouples_df[thermocouples_df['run_id'] == run_id].copy()\n\n        # Select thermocouple at largest_mass location (most representative)\n        largest_mass_data = run_data[run_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n        if largest_mass_data.empty:\n            # If no largest_mass TC, skip this run\n            continue\n\n        # Get the first TC ID (should be consistent for a location)\n        tc_id = largest_mass_data['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n\n        # Filter data in preheat range (100°C to 150°C)\n        preheat_data = largest_mass_data[\n            (largest_mass_data['temp_c'] >= PREHEAT_START_TEMP_C) &\n            (largest_mass_data['temp_c'] <= PREHEAT_END_TEMP_C)\n        ].sort_values('time_s')\n\n        if len(preheat_data) < 2:\n            # Not enough data in preheat range\n            continue\n\n        # Calculate ramp rates between consecutive points\n        max_ramp = 0.0\n        for i in range(1, len(preheat_data)):\n            delta_t_s = preheat_data.iloc[i]['time_s'] - preheat_data.iloc[i-1]['time_s']\n            if delta_t_s > 0:\n                delta_temp_c = preheat_data.iloc[i]['temp_c'] - preheat_data.iloc[i-1]['temp_c']\n                ramp_rate = delta_temp_c / delta_t_s\n                max_ramp = max(max_ramp, ramp_rate)\n\n        results[\"max_ramp_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n            \"max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s\": round(max_ramp, 2)\n        }\n\n        # Check if violating\n        if max_ramp > PREHEAT_RAMP_LIMIT_C_PER_S:\n            results[\"violating_runs\"].append(run_id)\n\n    # Sort violating runs\n    results[\"violating_runs\"].sort()\n\n    return results\n\ndef calculate_time_above_liquidus(thermocouples_df: pd.DataFrame, mes_log_df: pd.DataFrame) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 2: Calculate time above liquidus (TAL) for each run.\n\n    TAL is the time spent above the solder liquidus temperature.\n    From handbook: wetting time is normally 30-60 seconds (lines 230-231).\n    Liquidus temperature is obtained from mes_log (solder_liquidus_c).\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n\n    # Get unique run IDs\n    run_ids = sorted(thermocouples_df['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in run_ids:\n        # Get liquidus temperature for this run\n        run_mes = mes_log_df[mes_log_df['run_id'] == run_id]\n        if run_mes.empty:\n            continue\n\n        liquidus_c = run_mes['solder_liquidus_c'].iloc[0]\n\n        # Get thermocouple data\n        run_data = thermocouples_df[thermocouples_df['run_id'] == run_id].copy()\n\n        # Select thermocouple at largest_mass location (most representative)\n        largest_mass_data = run_data[run_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n        if largest_mass_data.empty:\n            continue\n\n        tc_id = largest_mass_data['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n        largest_mass_data = largest_mass_data.sort_values('time_s')\n\n        # Find time above liquidus\n        above_liquidus = largest_mass_data[largest_mass_data['temp_c'] > liquidus_c]\n\n        if len(above_liquidus) < 2:\n            tal_s = 0.0\n        else:\n            # TAL is the time from first crossing to last crossing\n            tal_s = above_liquidus['time_s'].max() - above_liquidus['time_s'].min()\n\n        # Determine compliance\n        status = \"compliant\" if (MIN_TAL_S <= tal_s <= MAX_TAL_S) else \"non-compliant\"\n\n        results.append({\n            \"run_id\": str(run_id),\n            \"tc_id\": str(tc_id),\n            \"tal_s\": round(float(tal_s), 2),\n            \"required_min_tal_s\": float(MIN_TAL_S),\n            \"required_max_tal_s\": float(MAX_TAL_S),\n            \"status\": status\n        })\n\n    return results\n\ndef check_peak_temperature(thermocouples_df: pd.DataFrame, mes_log_df: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 3: Check if each run meets peak temperature requirement.\n\n    From handbook: temperature must exceed melting point by ~20°C (line 228).\n    Minimum peak = liquidus + 20°C\n    \"\"\"\n    results = {\n        \"failing_runs\": [],\n        \"min_peak_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    # Get all run IDs from mes_log\n    all_run_ids = sorted(mes_log_df['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_run_ids:\n        # Get liquidus temperature for this run\n        run_mes = mes_log_df[mes_log_df['run_id'] == run_id]\n        liquidus_c = run_mes['solder_liquidus_c'].iloc[0]\n        required_min_peak = liquidus_c + MIN_PEAK_OFFSET_C\n\n        # Get thermocouple data\n        run_data = thermocouples_df[thermocouples_df['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if run_data.empty:\n            # No thermocouple data = failing\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": None,\n                \"peak_temp_c\": None,\n                \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n            }\n            continue\n\n        # Select thermocouple at largest_mass location\n        largest_mass_data = run_data[run_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n        if largest_mass_data.empty:\n            # No largest_mass TC = failing\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": None,\n                \"peak_temp_c\": None,\n                \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n            }\n            continue\n\n        tc_id = largest_mass_data['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n        peak_temp = largest_mass_data['temp_c'].max()\n\n        results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n            \"peak_temp_c\": round(peak_temp, 2),\n            \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n        }\n\n        # Check if failing\n        if peak_temp < required_min_peak:\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n\n    return results\n\ndef check_conveyor_speed(mes_log_df: pd.DataFrame) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 4: Check if conveyor speed is feasible.\n\n    From handbook page 14-15 (lines 874-893):\n    LINE SPEED (MINIMUM) = BOARDS PER MINUTE × LENGTH PER BOARD / LOAD FACTOR\n    PROCESS SPEED = OVEN CHAMBER HEATED LENGTH / PROCESS DWELL TIME\n\n    The actual conveyor speed must be >= minimum line speed to avoid jams.\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n\n    # Need to determine required minimum speed based on thermal dwell requirements\n    # From handbook: typical process with 3 zones (preheat, dryout, reflow)\n    # Total dwell time can be estimated from the thermocouple data\n\n    for _, row in mes_log_df.iterrows():\n        run_id = row['run_id']\n\n        # Get board geometry\n        board_length_cm = row['board_length_cm']\n        boards_per_min = row['boards_per_min']\n        loading_factor = row['loading_factor']\n        actual_speed_cm_min = row['conveyor_speed_cm_min']\n\n        # Calculate minimum required conveyor speed\n        # This is based on preventing board jams at the entrance\n        required_min_speed = (boards_per_min * board_length_cm) / loading_factor\n\n        # Check if actual speed meets requirement\n        meets = actual_speed_cm_min >= required_min_speed\n\n        results.append({\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"required_min_speed_cm_min\": round(required_min_speed, 2),\n            \"actual_speed_cm_min\": round(actual_speed_cm_min, 2),\n            \"meets\": meets\n        })\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    return results\n\ndef select_best_runs(mes_log_df: pd.DataFrame, test_defects_df: pd.DataFrame,\n                     thermocouples_df: pd.DataFrame) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 5: Select best run per board_family based on quality and efficiency.\n\n    Criteria for \"best\":\n    1. Quality: highest yield (fp_yield_pct from test_defects SUMMARY rows)\n    2. Efficiency: fastest conveyor speed (secondary criterion)\n    3. Process compliance: meets temperature requirements\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n\n    # Get yield data from test defects\n    yield_data = test_defects_df[test_defects_df['inspection_stage'] == 'SUMMARY'][\n        ['run_id', 'fp_yield_pct']\n    ].copy()\n\n    # Merge with mes_log to get board_family and conveyor_speed\n    merged = mes_log_df.merge(yield_data, on='run_id', how='left')\n\n    # Group by board_family\n    board_families = sorted(merged['board_family'].unique())\n\n    for board_family in board_families:\n        family_runs = merged[merged['board_family'] == board_family].copy()\n\n        # Fill NaN yields with 0 (if no test data)\n        family_runs['fp_yield_pct'] = family_runs['fp_yield_pct'].fillna(0)\n\n        # Sort by:\n        # 1. Yield (descending)\n        # 2. Conveyor speed (descending - faster is better for efficiency)\n        family_runs = family_runs.sort_values(\n            by=['fp_yield_pct', 'conveyor_speed_cm_min'],\n            ascending=[False, False]\n        )\n\n        # Best run is the first one\n        best_run_id = family_runs.iloc[0]['run_id']\n\n        # All other runs are runner-ups\n        runner_up_run_ids = sorted(family_runs.iloc[1:]['run_id'].tolist())\n\n        results.append({\n            \"board_family\": board_family,\n            \"best_run_id\": best_run_id,\n            \"runner_up_run_ids\": runner_up_run_ids\n        })\n\n    # Sort by board_family\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['board_family'])\n\n    return results\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n Not enough data in preheat range\n            continue\n\n        # Calculate ramp rates between consecutive points\n        max_ramp = 0.0\n        for i in range(1, len(preheat_data)):\n            delta_t_s = preheat_data.iloc[i]['time_s'] - preheat_data.iloc[i-1]['time_s']\n            if delta_t_s > 0:\n                delta_temp_c = preheat_data.iloc[i]['temp_c'] - preheat_data.iloc[i-1]['temp_c']\n                ramp_rate = delta_temp_c / delta_t_s\n                max_ramp = max(max_ramp, ramp_rate)\n\n        results[\"max_ramp_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": str(tc_id),\n            \"max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s\": round(float(max_ramp), 2)\n        }\n\n        # Check if violating\n        if max_ramp > PREHEAT_RAMP_LIMIT_C_PER_S:\n            results[\"violating_runs\"].append(str(run_id))\n\n    # Sort violating runs\n    results[\"violating_runs\"].sort()\n\n    return results\n\ndef calculate_time_above_liquidus(thermocouples_df: pd.DataFrame, mes_log_df: pd.DataFrame) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 2: Calculate time above liquidus (TAL) for each run.\n\n    TAL is the time spent above the solder liquidus temperature.\n    From handbook: wetting time is normally 30-60 seconds (lines 230-231).\n    Liquidus temperature is obtained from mes_log (solder_liquidus_c).\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n\n    # Get unique run IDs\n    run_ids = sorted(thermocouples_df['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in run_ids:\n        # Get liquidus temperature for this run\n        run_mes = mes_log_df[mes_log_df['run_id'] == run_id]\n        if run_mes.empty:\n            continue\n\n        liquidus_c = run_mes['solder_liquidus_c'].iloc[0]\n\n        # Get thermocouple data\n        run_data = thermocouples_df[thermocouples_df['run_id'] == run_id].copy()\n\n        # Select thermocouple at largest_mass location (most representative)\n        largest_mass_data = run_data[run_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n        if largest_mass_data.empty:\n            continue\n\n        tc_id = largest_mass_data['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n        largest_mass_data = largest_mass_data.sort_values('time_s')\n\n        # Find time above liquidus\n        above_liquidus = largest_mass_data[largest_mass_data['temp_c'] > liquidus_c]\n\n        if len(above_liquidus) < 2:\n            tal_s = 0.0\n        else:\n            # TAL is the time from first crossing to last crossing\n            tal_s = above_liquidus['time_s'].max() - above_liquidus['time_s'].min()\n\n        # Determine compliance\n        status = \"compliant\" if (MIN_TAL_S <= tal_s <= MAX_TAL_S) else \"non-compliant\"\n\n        results.append({\n            \"run_id\": str(run_id),\n            \"tc_id\": str(tc_id),\n            \"tal_s\": round(float(tal_s), 2),\n            \"required_min_tal_s\": float(MIN_TAL_S),\n            \"required_max_tal_s\": float(MAX_TAL_S),\n            \"status\": status\n        })\n\n    return results\n\ndef check_peak_temperature(thermocouples_df: pd.DataFrame, mes_log_df: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 3: Check if each run meets peak temperature requirement.\n\n    From handbook: temperature must exceed melting point by ~20°C (line 228).\n    Minimum peak = liquidus + 20°C\n    \"\"\"\n    results = {\n        \"failing_runs\": [],\n        \"min_peak_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    # Get all run IDs from mes_log\n    all_run_ids = sorted(mes_log_df['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_run_ids:\n        # Get liquidus temperature for this run\n        run_mes = mes_log_df[mes_log_df['run_id'] == run_id]\n        liquidus_c = run_mes['solder_liquidus_c'].iloc[0]\n        required_min_peak = liquidus_c + MIN_PEAK_OFFSET_C\n\n        # Get thermocouple data\n        run_data = thermocouples_df[thermocouples_df['run_id'] == run_id]\n\n        if run_data.empty:\n            # No thermocouple data = failing\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": None,\n                \"peak_temp_c\": None,\n                \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n            }\n            continue\n\n        # Select thermocouple at largest_mass location\n        largest_mass_data = run_data[run_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n        if largest_mass_data.empty:\n            # No largest_mass TC = failing\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": None,\n                \"peak_temp_c\": None,\n                \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n            }\n            continue\n\n        tc_id = largest_mass_data['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n        peak_temp = largest_mass_data['temp_c'].max()\n\n        results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": tc_id,\n            \"peak_temp_c\": round(peak_temp, 2),\n            \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n        }\n\n        # Check if failing\n        if peak_temp < required_min_peak:\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n\n    return results\n\ndef check_conveyor_speed(mes_log_df: pd.DataFrame) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 4: Check if conveyor speed is feasible.\n\n    From handbook page 14-15 (lines 874-893):\n    LINE SPEED (MINIMUM) = BOARDS PER MINUTE × LENGTH PER BOARD / LOAD FACTOR\n    PROCESS SPEED = OVEN CHAMBER HEATED LENGTH / PROCESS DWELL TIME\n\n    The actual conveyor speed must be >= minimum line speed to avoid jams.\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n\n    # Need to determine required minimum speed based on thermal dwell requirements\n    # From handbook: typical process with 3 zones (preheat, dryout, reflow)\n    # Total dwell time can be estimated from the thermocouple data\n\n    for _, row in mes_log_df.iterrows():\n        run_id = row['run_id']\n\n        # Get board geometry\n        board_length_cm = row['board_length_cm']\n        boards_per_min = row['boards_per_min']\n        loading_factor = row['loading_factor']\n        actual_speed_cm_min = row['conveyor_speed_cm_min']\n\n        # Calculate minimum required conveyor speed\n        # This is based on preventing board jams at the entrance\n        required_min_speed = (boards_per_min * board_length_cm) / loading_factor\n\n        # Check if actual speed meets requirement\n        meets = actual_speed_cm_min >= required_min_speed\n\n        results.append({\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"required_min_speed_cm_min\": round(required_min_speed, 2),\n            \"actual_speed_cm_min\": round(actual_speed_cm_min, 2),\n            \"meets\": meets\n        })\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    return results\n\ndef select_best_runs(mes_log_df: pd.DataFrame, test_defects_df: pd.DataFrame,\n                     thermocouples_df: pd.DataFrame) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 5: Select best run per board_family based on quality and efficiency.\n\n    Criteria for \"best\":\n    1. Quality: highest yield (fp_yield_pct from test_defects SUMMARY rows)\n    2. Efficiency: fastest conveyor speed (secondary criterion)\n    3. Process compliance: meets temperature requirements\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n\n    # Get yield data from test defects\n    yield_data = test_defects_df[test_defects_df['inspection_stage'] == 'SUMMARY'][\n        ['run_id', 'fp_yield_pct']\n    ].copy()\n\n    # Merge with mes_log to get board_family and conveyor_speed\n    merged = mes_log_df.merge(yield_data, on='run_id', how='left')\n\n    # Group by board_family\n    board_families = sorted(merged['board_family'].unique())\n\n    for board_family in board_families:\n        family_runs = merged[merged['board_family'] == board_family].copy()\n\n        # Fill NaN yields with 0 (if no test data)\n        family_runs['fp_yield_pct'] = family_runs['fp_yield_pct'].fillna(0)\n\n        # Sort by:\n        # 1. Yield (descending)\n        # 2. Conveyor speed (descending - faster is better for efficiency)\n        family_runs = family_runs.sort_values(\n            by=['fp_yield_pct', 'conveyor_speed_cm_min'],\n            ascending=[False, False]\n        )\n\n        # Best run is the first one\n        best_run_id = family_runs.iloc[0]['run_id']\n\n        # All other runs are runner-ups\n        runner_up_run_ids = sorted(family_runs.iloc[1:]['run_id'].tolist())\n\n        results.append({\n            \"board_family\": board_family,\n            \"best_run_id\": best_run_id,\n            \"runner_up_run_ids\": runner_up_run_ids\n        })\n\n    # Sort by board_family\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['board_family'])\n\n    return results\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n From handbook line 214\nPREHEAT_START_TEMP_C = 100  # From handbook line 212\nPREHEAT_END_TEMP_C = 150    # From handbook line 212\nMIN_TAL_S = 30  # From handbook line 231\nMAX_TAL_S = 60  # From handbook line 231\nMIN_PEAK_OFFSET_C = 20  # From handbook line 228\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load all data files.\"\"\"\n    mes_log = pd.read_csv('/app/data/mes_log.csv')\n    thermocouples = pd.read_csv('/app/data/thermocouples.csv')\n    test_defects = pd.read_csv('/app/data/test_defects.csv')\n    return mes_log, thermocouples, test_defects\n\ndef calculate_preheat_ramp_rates(thermocouples_df: pd.DataFrame, mes_log_df: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 1: Calculate maximum preheat ramp rate for each run.\n\n    Preheat zone is defined as 100°C to 150°C (from handbook).\n    Ramp rate = ΔT / Δt in °C/second\n    Select thermocouple at \"largest_mass\" location (most representative per handbook).\n    \"\"\"\n    results = {\n        \"ramp_rate_limit_c_per_s\": PREHEAT_RAMP_LIMIT_C_PER_S,\n        \"violating_runs\": [],\n        \"max_ramp_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    # Get unique run IDs\n    run_ids = sorted(thermocouples_df['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in run_ids:\n        run_data = thermocouples_df[thermocouples_df['run_id'] == run_id].copy()\n\n        # Select thermocouple at largest_mass location (most representative)\n        largest_mass_data = run_data[run_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n        if largest_mass_data.empty:\n            # If no largest_mass TC, skip this run\n            continue\n\n        # Get the first TC ID (should be consistent for a location)\n        tc_id = largest_mass_data['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n\n        # Filter data in preheat range (100°C to 150°C)\n        preheat_data = largest_mass_data[\n            (largest_mass_data['temp_c'] >= PREHEAT_START_TEMP_C) &\n            (largest_mass_data['temp_c'] <= PREHEAT_END_TEMP_C)\n        ].sort_values('time_s')\n\n        if len(preheat_data) < 2:\n            # Not enough data in preheat range\n            continue\n\n        # Calculate ramp rates between consecutive points\n        max_ramp = 0.0\n        for i in range(1, len(preheat_data)):\n            delta_t_s = preheat_data.iloc[i]['time_s'] - preheat_data.iloc[i-1]['time_s']\n            if delta_t_s > 0:\n                delta_temp_c = preheat_data.iloc[i]['temp_c'] - preheat_data.iloc[i-1]['temp_c']\n                ramp_rate = delta_temp_c / delta_t_s\n                max_ramp = max(max_ramp, ramp_rate)\n\n        results[\"max_ramp_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": str(tc_id),\n            \"max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s\": round(float(max_ramp), 2)\n        }\n\n        # Check if violating\n        if max_ramp > PREHEAT_RAMP_LIMIT_C_PER_S:\n            results[\"violating_runs\"].append(str(run_id))\n\n    # Sort violating runs\n    results[\"violating_runs\"].sort()\n\n    return results\n\ndef calculate_time_above_liquidus(thermocouples_df: pd.DataFrame, mes_log_df: pd.DataFrame) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 2: Calculate time above liquidus (TAL) for each run.\n\n    TAL is the time spent above the solder liquidus temperature.\n    From handbook: wetting time is normally 30-60 seconds (lines 230-231).\n    Liquidus temperature is obtained from mes_log (solder_liquidus_c).\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n\n    # Get unique run IDs\n    run_ids = sorted(thermocouples_df['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in run_ids:\n        # Get liquidus temperature for this run\n        run_mes = mes_log_df[mes_log_df['run_id'] == run_id]\n        if run_mes.empty:\n            continue\n\n        liquidus_c = run_mes['solder_liquidus_c'].iloc[0]\n\n        # Get thermocouple data\n        run_data = thermocouples_df[thermocouples_df['run_id'] == run_id].copy()\n\n        # Select thermocouple at largest_mass location (most representative)\n        largest_mass_data = run_data[run_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']\n\n        if largest_mass_data.empty:\n            continue\n\n        tc_id = largest_mass_data['tc_id'].iloc[0]\n        largest_mass_data = largest_mass_data.sort_values('time_s')\n\n        # Find time above liquidus\n        above_liquidus = largest_mass_data[largest_mass_data['temp_c'] > liquidus_c]\n\n        if len(above_liquidus) < 2:\n            tal_s = 0.0\n        else:\n            # TAL is the time from first crossing to last crossing\n            tal_s = above_liquidus['time_s'].max() - above_liquidus['time_s'].min()\n\n        # Determine compliance\n        status = \"compliant\" if (MIN_TAL_S <= tal_s <= MAX_TAL_S) else \"non-compliant\"\n\n        results.append({\n            \"run_id\": str(run_id),\n            \"tc_id\": str(tc_id),\n            \"tal_s\": round(float(tal_s), 2),\n            \"required_min_tal_s\": float(MIN_TAL_S),\n            \"required_max_tal_s\": float(MAX_TAL_S),\n            \"status\": status\n        })\n\n    return results\n\ndef check_peak_temperature(thermocouples_df: pd.DataFrame, mes_log_df: pd.DataFrame) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 3: Check if each run meets peak temperature requirement.\n\n    From handbook: temperature must exceed melting point by ~20°C (line 228).\n    Minimum peak = liquidus + 20°C\n    \"\"\"\n    results = {\n        \"failing_runs\": [],\n        \"min_peak_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    # Check if failing\n        if peak_temp < required_min_peak:\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(str(run_id))\n\n    return results\n\ndef check_conveyor_speed(mes_log_df: pd.DataFrame) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 4: Check if conveyor speed is feasible.\n\n    From handbook page 14-15 (lines 874-893):\n    LINE SPEED (MINIMUM) = BOARDS PER MINUTE × LENGTH PER BOARD / LOAD FACTOR\n    PROCESS SPEED = OVEN CHAMBER HEATED LENGTH / PROCESS DWELL TIME\n\n    The actual conveyor speed must be >= minimum line speed to avoid jams.\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n\n    #
